<script>
    var __isp_options = {
      isp_serp_shopify_image_size: '431x539',
      isp_serp_callback: function() {
        /*
         * Change product title
         */
        $(".isp_grid_product").each(function() {
          var productColor = ISP_PRODUCTS[parseInt($(this).attr("product_id"))].vra[0][1][0][1][0];
          if (productColor.indexOf('(') != -1) {
          	productColor = productColor.substring(0, productColor.indexOf('('));
          }
          var productTitle = $(this).find(".isp_product_title").text();
          if (productTitle.indexOf('-') != -1) {
            productTitle = productTitle.substring(0, productTitle.indexOf(' -'));
          	$(this).find(".isp_product_title").text(productTitle);
            $(this).find(".isp_product_title").text(productTitle.concat(' - ' + productColor));
          }
        });

        /* Create div for dropdown filters */
        var div = $("<div id='full-width-filter'></div>");
        if ($('#full-width-filter').length == 0) {
          $(".isp_center_container_header").prepend(div);
		}

        /* Empty the filter div */
        $('#full-width-filter').empty();

        /* Create <select> for each filter */
        $("div.isp_single_facet_wrapper").each(function() {
          	var id = $(this).children().first().text().trim();
        	var select = $("<select class=dropDown id=" + id + "></select>");
          	(select).appendTo("#full-width-filter");
        });

        /* Create <option> for each <select> */
        $("div.isp_single_facet_wrapper").each(function(index) {
          var length = $(this).children().length;
          if (length < 7) {
            $(this).children().each(function() {
              var text = $(this).text().trim();
              if (text.substr(0, text.indexOf('('))) {
              	text = text.substr(0, text.indexOf('('));
              }
              var value = $(this).children().eq(0).attr("facet_value");
              if (text.indexOf("Any Category") > -1) {
                var option = $("<option value='any_category'>" + text + "</option>");
              } else if ($(this).attr("class") == "isp_facet_narrowed_by_category") {
                var option = $("<option selected>" + text + "</option>");
              } else if ($("a[facet_value='" + value + "']").children().eq(0).attr("class") == "isp_facet_check_box_icon isp_facet_check_box_icon_checked") {
                var option = $("<option value='" + value + "' selected>" + text + "</option>");
              } else {
                var option = $("<option value=" + value + ">" + text + "</option>");
              }
              option.appendTo($("#full-width-filter").children().eq(index));
            });
          } else {
            $(this).children().each(function() {
              if ($(this).attr("class") == "isp_facet_value" || $(this).attr("class") == "isp_facet_value isp_facet_value_color_swatch") {
                var text = $(this).text().trim();
                if (text.substr(0, text.indexOf('('))) {
                  text = text.substr(0, text.indexOf('('));
                }
                var value = $(this).children().eq(0).attr("facet_value");
                if (text.indexOf("Any Category") > -1) {
                	var option = $("<option value='any_category'>" + text + "</option>");
                } else if ($(this).attr("class") == "isp_facet_narrowed_by_category") {
                  var option = $("<option selected>" + text + "</option>");
                } else if ($("a[facet_value='" + value + "']").children().eq(0).attr("class") == "isp_facet_check_box_icon isp_facet_check_box_icon_checked" || $("a[facet_value='" + value + "']").children().eq(0).attr("class") == "isp_facet_check_box_icon isp_facet_check_box_color_swatch isp_facet_check_box_icon_checked") {
                  var option = $("<option value='" + value + "' selected>" + text + "</option>");
                } else {
                  var option = $("<option value=" + value + ">" + text + "</option>");
                }
                option.appendTo($("#full-width-filter").children().eq(index));
              } else {
              	$(this).children().each(function() {
                  var text = $(this).text().trim();
                  if (text.substr(0, text.indexOf('('))) {
                    text = text.substr(0, text.indexOf('('));
                  }
                  var value = $(this).children().eq(0).attr("facet_value");
                  if (text.indexOf("Any Category") > -1) {
                  	var option = $("<option value='any_category'>" + text + "</option>");
                  } else if ($(this).attr("class") == "isp_facet_narrowed_by_category") {
                    var option = $("<option selected>" + text + "</option>");
                  } else if ($("a[facet_value='" + value + "']").children().eq(0).attr("class") == "isp_facet_check_box_icon isp_facet_check_box_icon_checked" || $("a[facet_value='" + value + "']").children().eq(0).attr("class") == "isp_facet_check_box_icon isp_facet_check_box_color_swatch isp_facet_check_box_icon_checked") {
                    var option = $("<option value='" + value + "' selected>" + text + "</option>");
                  } else {
                    var option = $("<option value=" + value + ">" + text + "</option>");
                  }
                  option.appendTo($("#full-width-filter").children().eq(index));
                });
              }
            });
          }
        });

        /* Create sort <select> */
        var sortSelect = $("<select class=sortDropDown id=sortID></select>");
        (sortSelect).appendTo("#full-width-filter");

        /* Create <option>s for sort <select> */
        $("ul#sorting_drop_list").children().each(function() {
          var text = $(this).text().trim();
          var value = $(this).children().eq(0).attr("id");
          if ($("a#" + value).attr("class") == "selected_sorting_value") {
          	var option = $("<option value='" + value + "' selected>" + text + "</option>");
          }
          else {
            var option = $("<option value=" + value + ">" + text + "</option>");
          }
          (option).appendTo($("#full-width-filter").find("#sortID"));
        });

        /* Clickable dropdown */
        $(".dropDown").change(function(event) {
          	var optionName = this.id;
          	var optionID = this.value;
            if (optionID == "any_category") {
              $("div#isp_left_container a[facet_name='any_category']").click();
            }
            else {
              $("div#isp_left_container a[facet_name=" + optionName + "][facet_value='" + optionID + "']").click();
            }
        });

        /* Clickable sort dropdown */
        $(".sortDropDown").change(function(event) {
          	var optionID = this.value;
          	$("div.isp_sorting_and_result_view_wrapper a[id='" + optionID + "']").click();
        });

        /* Remove empty <option> from price */
        $("#Price option[value=undefined]:not(:first)").remove();

        /* Pagination to top */
        $(".isp_results_summary_wrapper").append($("#isp_pagination_anchor"));

        /* Add filter & sort by text */
        var p1 = $("<p class='filter-by'>Filter by</p>");
        $("#full-width-filter").prepend(p1);
        var p2 = $("<p class='filter-sort-by-title'>Sort by</p>");
        $("#Price").after(p2);

        /* Mobile CSS */
        $("li.isp_grid_product").addClass("mobile-half");
        if ($(window).width() < 739) {
        	$("#isp_search_results_container").after($(".isp_results_summary_wrapper"));
          	var btnFilterPlus = $("<button class='btnFilterPlus'>+</button>");
          	var btnFilterMinus = $("<button class='btnFilterMinus' style='display: none;'>-</button>");
          	$(".filter-by").after($(btnFilterPlus));
          	$(btnFilterPlus).after($(btnFilterMinus));
            $(".btnFilterPlus").click(function(e) {
                $(".btnFilterPlus").css("display", "none");
                $(".btnFilterMinus").css("display", "block");
                $(".dropDown").css("display", "block");
              	$("#isp_search_results_container").css("margin-top", "85px");
            });
         	$(".btnFilterMinus").click(function(e) {
                $(".btnFilterPlus").css("display", "block");
                $(".btnFilterMinus").css("display", "none");
                $(".dropDown").css("display", "none");
              	$("#isp_search_results_container").css("margin-top", "0px");
            });
          	$(".dropDown").wrapAll("<div class='collection-filter-by-categories'/>");
            $(".dropDown").change(function(event) {
                var optionName = this.id;
              	if (optionName == "Color" || optionName == "Size" || optionName == "Price") {
                	$("a[facet_name=isp_clear_all_facets]").click();
                }
            });
        }
      },
  	  isp_serp_no_results_callback: function() {
          $jquery_isp('.isp_grid_product').each(function() {
             if ($jquery_isp(this).attr('class').indexOf('mobile-half') == -1) {
                 $jquery_isp(this).addClass('mobile-half');
             }
          });
      }
    }
  </script>

<script>
(function(){
  var calculateSubmenuHeight = function() {
    $('#nav-links .dropdown .submenu').each(function(index, value) {
      var height = $(this).height();
      $(this).find('> div').first()[0].className = "";
      $(this).find('> div *[class^="desktop-"]').each(function() { this.className = ""; });
      var blackground = $(this).next('.greenscreen').length ? $(this).next('.greenscreen') : $('<div>').addClass('greenscreen');
      $(this).addClass('aligned').after(blackground.css('height', height));
    }); 
  };
  calculateSubmenuHeight();
  
  // recalculate on window.resize
  $(window).resize(function() {
    clearTimeout(window.resizedFinished);
    window.resizedFinished = setTimeout(function(){
        calculateSubmenuHeight();
    }, 250);
  });
}());
</script>

       $('.rotatee').toggleClass("downyy");
  
});

<script type="text/javascript">
(function(){

function priceFromCents(cents) {
    if (typeof(cents) != "number")
        return;

    return (cents / 100);
}

var deviceType = /iPad/.test(navigator.userAgent) ? "t" : /Mobile|iP(hone|od)|Android|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Silk/.test(navigator.userAgent) ? "m" : "d";
window.criteo_q = window.criteo_q || [];
window.criteo_q.push(
  { event: "setAccount", account: 54010 },
  { event: "setEmail", email: "" },
  { event: "setSiteType", type: deviceType },
  { event: "viewHome" }
  
);

function isRelevantCartUpdate(settings) {
    function dataContains(term) {
        return settings.data && settings.data.indexOf && settings.data.indexOf(term) > -1;
    }

    return (settings.url == "/cart/add.js" || settings.url == "/cart/update.js")
      && (
        dataContains('attributes[prod') ||
        (dataContains('id=') && dataContains('quantity='))
      );
}

$(document).ajaxSuccess(function(event, xhr, settings) {
    if (isRelevantCartUpdate(settings)) {
        $.getJSON("/cart.js", function(data) {
            var basketData = [];
            var item;

            if (!data.items || !data.items.length)
                return;

            for (var i = 0; i < data.items.length; i++) {
                item = data.items[i];

                basketData.push({
                    id: "shopify_US_" + item.product_id + "_" + item.variant_id,
                    price: priceFromCents(item.price),
                    quantity: item.quantity
                });
            }

            window.criteo_q.push({
                event: "viewBasket",
                product: basketData
            });
        });
    }
});
})();
</script>

        }   
      });

           event: "viewBasket",                     
           product: basketdata                
         });             
      });     
    }     
  }); 
</script>
